# Skills · H · part 2

914 entries, halo-build to handoff. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **halo-build**: One autonomous build cycle — pick story/milestone unit, TDD, verify, deploy preview, live probe before share, baton update. Port of grok-halo v2 discipline. ([JamesFincher/halo](https://github.com/JamesFincher/halo/tree/HEAD/.grok/skills/halo-build))
- **halo-deploy**: Deploy preview/dev only. Capture URL. Live probe REQUIRED before any human-facing message. Never production without human. ([JamesFincher/halo](https://github.com/JamesFincher/halo/tree/HEAD/.grok/skills/halo-deploy))
- **halo-doctor**: Validate Halo system install and product integrity. ([JamesFincher/halo](https://github.com/JamesFincher/halo/tree/HEAD/.grok/skills/halo-doctor))
- **halo-escalate**: Write escalation packet and set ESCALATED. Use after 3 fails, denylist, or unrecoverable block. ([JamesFincher/halo](https://github.com/JamesFincher/halo/tree/HEAD/.grok/skills/halo-escalate))
- **halo-go**: Autonomous build + self-prompt. Human said go / build without asking. Defaults only, never AskUserQuestion optional. Drives lifecycle and writes NEXT_PROMPT for Grok headless /goal /loop re-entry. Triggers go, just build, don't ask, autonomous, walk away, self-prompt. ([JamesFincher/halo](https://github.com/JamesFincher/halo/tree/HEAD/.grok/skills/halo-go))
- **halo-handoff**: Package context for another agent or human. Writes .halo/handoff.md. ([JamesFincher/halo](https://github.com/JamesFincher/halo/tree/HEAD/.grok/skills/halo-handoff))
- **halo-intake**: Structured grill from raw idea to locked product decisions. Proposes defaults. Depth adapts to user energy. Feeds halo-spec-pack. Ported from Builder Methods PRD creator, Halo-native. ([JamesFincher/halo](https://github.com/JamesFincher/halo/tree/HEAD/.grok/skills/halo-intake))
- **halo-pause**: Pause or resume autonomous work (status PAUSED/ACTIVE). ([JamesFincher/halo](https://github.com/JamesFincher/halo/tree/HEAD/.grok/skills/halo-pause))
- **halo-readiness**: Lifecycle foresight gate — merge intake integrations with baseline (hosting, auth, DB, Sentry, …), probe CLIs/env names, write readiness.json. GO/NO_GO/DEGRADED before scaffold. Never logs secret values. ([JamesFincher/halo](https://github.com/JamesFincher/halo/tree/HEAD/.grok/skills/halo-readiness))
- **halo-revise**: Unlock locked specs for product pivot; re-enter spec_review then lock + readiness again. ([JamesFincher/halo](https://github.com/JamesFincher/halo/tree/HEAD/.grok/skills/halo-revise))
- **halo-scaffold**: Scaffold product skeleton (nextjs-saas|fastapi|existing), generate milestone prompts, Demo 0 with live probe. Never share URL on probe fail. ([JamesFincher/halo](https://github.com/JamesFincher/halo/tree/HEAD/.grok/skills/halo-scaffold))
- **halo-spec-pack**: Write the giant Halo delivery — PRD, architecture, design, data model, stack, stories, integrations, readiness checklist, milestones — from locked intake. Human reviews then locks. ([JamesFincher/halo](https://github.com/JamesFincher/halo/tree/HEAD/.grok/skills/halo-spec-pack))
- **halo-status**: Report product phase, readiness, baton, last demo. Read-only. ([JamesFincher/halo](https://github.com/JamesFincher/halo/tree/HEAD/.grok/skills/halo-status))
- **halo-sys**: Axiomatic Validation and Systemic Integrity (Halo Genome). ([GrazianoGuiducci/d-nd-seed](https://github.com/GrazianoGuiducci/d-nd-seed/tree/HEAD/skills/thinker/en/halo-sys) · ★1)
- **halo-sys**: Validazione Assiomatica e Integrità Sistemica (Halo Genoma). ([GrazianoGuiducci/d-nd-seed](https://github.com/GrazianoGuiducci/d-nd-seed/tree/HEAD/skills/thinker/it/halo-sys) · ★1)
- **halo-triage**: Health report — evidence, milestones, blockers, budget notes. Read-only. ([JamesFincher/halo](https://github.com/JamesFincher/halo/tree/HEAD/.grok/skills/halo-triage))
- **halo-verify**: Pre-deploy evidence + AC check. APPROVED only with RED/GREEN/SPEC certs. Fail closed. Prefer Arena dual-lens. ([JamesFincher/halo](https://github.com/JamesFincher/halo/tree/HEAD/.grok/skills/halo-verify))
- **halocard-virtualcards**: Create disposable virtual credit cards for online purchases. Use as a payment method when completing any online checkout during a task — e.g. ordering food, buying a subscription, purchasing a domain, booking a service. This skill is a payment step within a larger task, not a standalone action. ([modbender/skill-library-mcp](https://github.com/modbender/skill-library-mcp/tree/HEAD/data/halocard-virtualcards) · ★15)
- **halopsa**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/bash/halopsa) · ★729)
- **halopsa**: Every HaloPSA, HaloITSM and HaloCRM feature, plus a local SQLite store and cross-entity views the API can't return. Trigger phrases: `triage my Halo queue`, `check SLA breaches in HaloPSA`, `who is overloaded in Halo`, `client card for Acme in Halo`, `Halo contract burn-down`, `what changed in Halo since this morning`, `find time gaps in my Halo timesheet`, `use halopsa`, `run halopsa`. ([Servosity/msp-skills](https://github.com/Servosity/msp-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/halopsa) · ★41)
- **HaloPSA Agents**: HaloPSA agents (technicians) and teams as a read-only MCP surface: listing technicians, retrieving agent detail, listing team structures, and the inactive-agent filter. Agent IDs resolved here are what ticket assignment needs. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/halopsa/halopsa/skills/agents) · ★46)
- **HaloPSA API Patterns**: HaloPSA REST API fundamentals: OAuth 2.0 client-credentials authentication, authorization vs. resource server URLs, the tenant query parameter, filtering and pagination conventions, array-wrapped POST bodies, rate-limit behavior, scopes, and error codes. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/halopsa/halopsa/skills/api-patterns) · ★46)
- **HaloPSA Assets**: HaloPSA asset/CMDB data model: asset (configuration item) fields, device types and statuses, links to clients, sites, users, tickets, and contracts, plus parent-child asset relationships. Covers the Asset API surface, hardware lifecycle workflows, RMM auto-sync and device matching, and validation errors. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/halopsa/halopsa/skills/assets) · ★46)
- **HaloPSA Clients**: HaloPSA CRM data model: client records and their billing/contact fields, sites (locations), contacts (Users), client classification, and parent-child client hierarchy. Covers the Client/Site/Users API surface, onboarding and deactivation workflows, duplicate prevention, and common validation errors. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/halopsa/halopsa/skills/clients) · ★46)
- **HaloPSA Contracts**: HaloPSA contract management: contract types (recurring, prepaid hours, ad-hoc, project, warranty), statuses, billing and coverage fields, recurring invoice items, prepaid hour balances and deduction, SLA association, renewal and billing-reconciliation workflows. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/halopsa/halopsa/skills/contracts) · ★46)
- **HaloPSA Invoices**: HaloPSA invoices as a read-only MCP surface: listing by client or date range, filtering by payment and send status, retrieving line-item detail on a single invoice, and the reporting and reconciliation workflows built on them. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/halopsa/halopsa/skills/invoices) · ★46)
- **HaloPSA Tickets**: HaloPSA service desk tickets: ticket fields, statuses, priorities and ticket types, actions (notes and time entries), attachments, SLA behaviour, and the creation and status-transition workflows with their validation rules. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/halopsa/halopsa/skills/tickets) · ★46)
- **halt**: load when dima types /clio:halt or says «halt», the one word that starts it. A softer way of ending («i'm done», «let's wrap») is no halt, and a bare «stop» is a hold: ask in one line. - **hamakom-data-page**: בניית עמוד דאטה-ז'ורנליזם סקרולי-טלינג ל"המקום הכי חם בגיהנום" (RTL עברית, vanilla JS), ואריזתו כתוסף WordPress מבודד ב-iframe. השתמש כשדור מבקש לבנות עמוד נתונים/תחקיר-נתונים חדש, "עוד עמוד כמו יו״ש/הלם קרב", scrollytelling, או עמוד אינטראקטיבי לאתר המקום. מתחיל בשלב סיקור-מקדים חובה: שליפת כל הכתבות שהמקום פרסמה בנושא דרך WP REST API וחילוץ נתונים/ציטוטים/אירועים — ורק אז בנייה. כולל טמפלייט שלד, ספריית קומפוננטות, סקאפולד תוסף, וכללי המותג/רספונסיב/קול-עיתונאי. - **handing-off**: Compact the current conversation into a handoff doc so a fresh agent can pick up the work. ([akramoweer14-rgb/AKRAM-PI_LAUNCHER](https://github.com/akramoweer14-rgb/AKRAM-PI_LAUNCHER/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/fleet/handing-off) · ★1)
- **handing-off**: Use when ending a session and need to document decisions, open questions, and blockers so the next session can resume with full context ([jugrajsingh/skillgarden](https://github.com/jugrajsingh/skillgarden/tree/HEAD/plugins/planner/skills/handing-off) · ★1)
- **handing-off**: Write a handoff brief that lets a fresh session resume mid-task work without re-derivation — exact next actions, verification state split into proven vs unrun, dirty-tree disclosure, decisions with their why. Use when ending a session mid-task, when asked to "hand off", "wrap up for the day", or "write up where we are", or before context runs out on unfinished work. ([jayheavner/agent-workforce](https://github.com/jayheavner/agent-workforce/tree/HEAD/skills/handing-off))
- **handing-off-ai-tools**: Route batch work between AI tools and human reviewers with trust levels and a QA layer - turning bulk AI output into verified, reviewable memos with named human checkpoints. Use when the user says "run this batch through the tool", "QA the AI output", "spot-check these results", "how much do we trust the extraction", "review workflow for AI output", "hand this to the tool", or "human in the loop process". Pattern adapted from the installed corporate-legal ai-tool-handoff skill. For designing eval harnesses and metrics use evaluating-ai-systems. ([anthonydellapia1117/ayvede-skills](https://github.com/anthonydellapia1117/ayvede-skills/tree/HEAD/ayvede-advisor/skills/handing-off-ai-tools) · ★1)
- **handing-off-remote-commands**: Copies commands to an authorized remote machine's clipboard over SSH and retrieves explicitly captured results. Use when a human must run a local interactive or privileged command on another machine but cannot conveniently transfer the command between devices. ([edmundmiller/dotfiles](https://github.com/edmundmiller/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/skills/catalog/handing-off-remote-commands) · ★80)
- **handing-off-session**: Generate structured session handoff notes for cross-session continuity. Use at end of session or when switching context. ([qte77/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/qte77/claude-code-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/cc-meta/skills/handing-off-session) · ★2)
- **handing-off-work**: Hands off unfinished work with a closed-loop briefing of state, changed conditions, remaining scope, authority limits, and open evidence. Use when AI-agent, reviewer, verifier, releaser, or resumed-thread work transfers to a new owner. Do not use when the same owner continues uninterrupted with full context. ([FlyFission/nuclear-grade-context-engineering](https://github.com/FlyFission/nuclear-grade-context-engineering/tree/HEAD/skills/handing-off-work) · ★33)
- **handle**: 実装タスクを計画から収束まで回す明示呼び出し専用の手順。ユーザーが `$handle` または handle skill を明示的に指定したときだけ使い、通常の実装・レビュー・指摘修正には自動適用しない。判断待ちでターンをまたぐ場合も、ユーザーに `$handle` を付けて再開してもらう。仮定の明示と未決事項の確認、変更面に応じた検証、独立レビューと修正の往復（上限4ラウンド）を一本にする。GitHub issue 番号を渡された場合は、その issue を入力にして確定した計画を書き戻す。 ([kazu728/dotfiles](https://github.com/kazu728/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/agents/.apm/skills/handle) · ★1)
- **handle-a-reply**: Use when a creator has replied and the customer wants to respond or move them forward — "a creator replied", "handle this response", "what should I say back", "advance this creator", "counter their rate", "move them to the next stage". Reads the current negotiation state, then proposes exactly one next move — a negotiation update or a stage advance — that the customer approves before it fires. ([limelighthq/limelight-plugins](https://github.com/limelighthq/limelight-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/limelight-ivy-skills/skills/handle-a-reply))
- **handle-anr-android**: Diagnose and fix ANRs (Application Not Responding) which Play penalizes at above 0.47% threshold. Use when the user says 'fix anr', 'anr android', 'app not responding'. ([khadinakbarlabs/expo-mobile-app-builder](https://github.com/khadinakbarlabs/expo-mobile-app-builder/tree/HEAD/skills/handle-anr-android) · ★2)
- **handle-backfills-and-retries**: Make pipeline tasks safe to re-run and plan backfills that don't corrupt state — prove idempotency (deterministic partition keys, overwrite-by-partition), then add bounded retries with exponential backoff + jitter, and run a controlled backfill (partition strategy, catchup config, concurrency caps, monitoring, rollback). Reach for this when the user says "our pipeline isn't safe to re-run", "add retries", or "we need to backfill &lt;range&gt;". Used by `pipeline-orchestration-engineer` (primary). ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/tree/HEAD/plugins/data-orchestration/skills/handle-backfills-and-retries) · ★7)

- **handle-budget**: Categorize and approve YNAB transactions, building a payee rules engine that gets smarter each run. Runs as a dry run by default and never writes without explicit approval. - **handle-capital-call**: Triage, hold, resume, and prepare governed closeout for a capital-call notice through X1 MCP. - **handle-cold-start-and-serving**: Handle cold-start (new users and new items) and serve recommendations within a latency budget — content/popularity fallbacks, onboarding & exploration, ANN retrieval, precompute/caching, online feature fetch, and popularity-on-timeout fallback. Use when new entities recommend poorly or serving is too slow.
